| # | Item | Verdict | Why, in one line |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Rock's roll-over reads as a dead pet | Applied | `happy` through the flip with the arc and blush kept upright on screen, the jaw at the stage minimum with the tongue out, the chin tucked 22°, a ±40° paddle on 4 f beats with bent knees and elbows, the tail wagging (4.3). |
| 2 | No brow on hungry, surprised, scared | Applied | The whole bar must fit inside the skull and is lowered, never dropped; the grow faces measure from the un-grown ring; no run under 3 columns (1.2). |
| 3 | Far legs vanish behind the near legs at walk keys | Applied | The far hip and shoulder slide half a stride outward through the walk (`shift`), the far hind stroke another 0.15 back: measured ≥ 4 px of every far leg at all 8 keys on all 18, soles still hold within 1 px (4.2 notes). |
| 4 | Spike's volley is a bundle | Applied | From the neck base within 4 f, forward quill first, 20° → 60° at 3 px/f, drawn behind the body and head; whole-pixel streaks; one sparkle per quill (3.3). |
| 5 | The baby happy notch hooks the "^" eye | Applied | Every mouth mark keeps ≥ 2 px of skin from the eye box; the baby pet opens its jaw 20° (2.5). |
| 6 | Baby slinkwing's E8 flop is a beret | Applied | The fans pivot from the crown's front and tip ~140°; the near lobe covers the eye (−110° still capped only its top half) (3.7). |
| 7 | Crumbs shrink to 1 px and scatter | Applied | 2 × 2 for life, then gone; they land within 8 px of the mouth (5.2). |
| 8 | Baby spike and baby rock asleep are one mound at /3 | Applied | Spike's baby raises its sleeping tail above level (nub over nub); the other tail-less-cue babies wrap theirs under (with #17), so rock is a round loaf and spike a raised wedge at all three phases. |
| 9 | The baby's open jaw is a sash across the chest | Applied | Hinge to tip 6.5 px and a 4 px drop (not 3: at 3 the mouth wedge was about 1 px), the tip within ~2 px under the snout (1.2). |

| 11 | Breath leaves above the mouth, before it opens | Applied | An open jaw's mouth anchor sits mid-opening; the jaw reaches its minimum by `cue` 0 (4.2 notes). |

| 16 | The baby beg eye presses the skull top; the egg tooth reads as a third catchlight | Applied | Baby tilt 14° (not 20°); the egg tooth is hidden while the jaw is shut in `hungry` (4.2). |
| 17 | The baby sleep bun's tail sticks straight back | Applied | Wrapped under where the cue is not on the tail (rock, lightning, slinkwing); fire's ember, water's paddle and spike's raised tail stay (4.2 notes). |
| 18 | Bible drift (fire's sleep, spike's fan) | Applied | 3.2 and 3.3 now say what the code does. |

**Found while fixing:**
- The walk's far legs could not simply be offset along the floor: a paw target ahead of the front leg's rest spot (or more than ~4 px behind the hind's) is out of reach, and the IK lifted it short, so far soles floated up to 3 px and skated up to 5 px. The `shift` channel moves the joint with the paw.
- `bowlFor` solves the chomp frame with the jaw shut, since an open jaw now moves the mouth anchor.
- The runtime blink swapped a `happy` "^" for an open, lidded eye for a few frames (and, upside down in the roll, a lid hanging from the eye's bottom): the blink now skips faces whose eyes are already shut (happy, closed, dazed).
- At a 16 % coverage threshold (stricter than 5.1 #14's 40 %) the audit flags anti-aliased toe fringes 1.2 to 1.5 px under the floor (19 to 25 % alpha) on a front paw's toe corner at rest and a lifting hind paw's tilted toe; they are in poses this round did not touch.
- Engine candidates: none new.

### Element pass
Each element was finished by its own artist in its own worktree (branch `elem/<id>`), critiqued, and fixed there, touching only its `elements/<id>.ts`: fire `e5252ed`, spike `7cda58c`, rock `a342021`, lightning `6390a80`, water `e8e69e8`, slinkwing `e833246` (its review-fix pass finished after a restart). The six files were integrated as they are; what each element now draws is in section 3 and 4.3, from the artists' notes. The artists' requests for SHARED changes were then applied or turned down, and the lead art director's own findings on the renders fixed, each looked at on game-scale renders before and after (lineup idle / walk / sleep, the silhouette sheet with its ÷ 3 blocks, the habitat and the habitat mix, every element's stage sheet, and the strips, zooms and casts named below); `tsc`, the palette check (277 / 277) and the smoke run (37 views) pass, and the floor audit, now over the element anims too (249 runs a seed), finds nothing sinking on seeds 1, 2, 3 and 5.

**Shared requests.**

| # | Request | From | Verdict | Why, in one line |
|---|---|---|---|---|

**The art director's findings (the renders, not the reports).**

| # | Found | Change | Before → after |
|---|---|---|---|
| a | The folded wing reads as a strap: a scale-coloured bar over a dark sliver | parts.ts `drawBatWing` and the folded angles of 2.2 (1.2, 1.3) | Before: every folded spar tip on one line under the arm, the membrane attached far back, a 1 to 2 px sliver under a 3 px bar, the knuckle lost in the corner of the neck. After: a round knob (young 3, adult 4 px) standing about 3 px over the back line clear behind the neck's root, the lead spar along the forearm as one 2 px leading edge to the rump, and under it a triangular panel of flat membrane 4 to 6 px deep with a lobed lower edge (the other fingers fan down to it, the attach moved under the shoulder). It reads as a folded wing at 1× on fire, slinkwing (a pink panel), water and spike (a leaf); rock's stays wholly under its dome, and lightning's cocked bolts are untouched. The panel's lower edge is held ≥ 5 px above the belly line on every body. |
| b | The sleep "z" reads as a dark block with a z in it | fx.ts glyph, rig.ts spawn (4.2, 5.2) | Before: ink strokes in a `belly` ring, 6 × 6 with 1-row counters, a dark tile at game scale (on the orange and olive bellies especially). After: a light 7 × 8 "z" in `#f8f4ec` with a thin 4-neighbour ink edge, 3 px open counters, rising from over the snout's tip, 8 px above the head: it reads as a floating letter, and it clears the baby slinkwing's ear on seeds 1, 2, 3 and 5. |
| c | Shared faults several reports agree on | | The backwards pet and beg clocks (#1, four reports), the colliding act ids (#2, three), the turn in place (#3, two) and the horns of a head turned back (#4, fire and spike) are the ones more than one artist hit; all four are fixed above. |

**Found while integrating.**
- Only the bones painted may join the wing's silhouette: the capsules wind against the membrane polygon, so the folded humerus and inner fingers (never painted) cut holes in the panel that showed their ink as scribbles; the folded panel is also flat (its shading gate now measures the membrane's own depth).
- A low fold (`foldRise` 0) keeps no downward fold shift: shifted down, rock's slate wing showed as a sliver under the dome's rim.
- The fold refits the body markings (2.7): where the panel covers the upper flank a haunch mark moves down or forward, and a seeded optional extra that no longer fits whole is dropped (young fire, seed 1).
- The gallery's frozen views step every pet in lockstep now (the wary latch reads the others), which changes nothing for a lone pet.
- Engine candidates: none new.

**Known gaps after the pass.**
- Nothing consumes `event: 'shriek'` / `'call'`; rock's beg pile is not a prop; the per-pet inputs are fed only by the gallery.
- The baby `surprised` / `scared` eye is an orange square with a 2 × 2 pupil (water's note).
- Slinkwing: at rest its ribs read as diverging strokes rather than fingers; the baby's rib looks a little like a gloss spot at mood 0; rib length shifts by one end stamp with the idle breath; its hand-mapped fan ribs would not follow a looked-back head (no slinkwing anim looks back).
- Fire: the flourish embers ride the tail's sway and the young and baby hops; the smoke tone is a derived colour no gate checks.
- Lightning: the adult's happy shower can put about 6 sparks in the air for a few frames (act flourishes are not budgeted); at the burst's middle step the adult's three sparks stack for 3 f; the baby's 5 × 5 tail Z reads as a blob.
- Water: happy spots read duller than resting ones (the open proposal in 3.6); the stream rides the head through the recover; the young's front flank carries no spot.
- Rock: the upset's front wall is a solid dark band and its eye window is tall (it clears the largest eye box).
- Spike: the baby's gauge and ripple are hard to see at game scale (half the adult range on 4 px nubs).

### Cast review round 1
Two reviews judged the whole cast together after the element pass: a silhouette and readability review of the ÷ 3 sheets, the lineups and the habitat (**S**: 0 blockers, 3 major, 7 minor) and an art-direction review of the strips, casts and zooms (**A**: 1 blocker, 2 major, 11 minor). Every item was reproduced on renders (`tools/shot.ts`: the ÷ 3 silhouette blocks, strips, zooms, casts, lineups and the habitat) before it was changed and looked at again after, across all six elements after every shared change; `tsc`, the palette check (277 / 277, re-pasted in 5.8 for rock's warmer sandstone) and the smoke run (38 views, the new leg-root audit included) pass, and the floor and leg-root audits find nothing on seeds 1, 2, 3 and 5.

| # | Item | Verdict | Why, in one line |
|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | **Blocker:** the far front leg floats free of the body in the walk (all six young, the water and lightning babies; fire's and slinkwing's adults join it at the throat) | Applied | The walk slid the far shoulder half a stride forward, past a young or baby chest's front edge. Its slide is now capped from the build's dims so the root disc stays min(3, its rest depth) px inside the chest or behind the near shoulder (3 to 5.5 px adults, 0 to 3.5 young, 0 or −0.5 babies: 4.2 notes); the far hind keeps its half stride back. New `view=roots` leg-root audit in the smoke run (5.1 #5): every far root ≥ 0.25 px deep on every walk, idle and rest frame (measured ≥ 0.5; the old walk −0.2 to −3.6). All 18 walk strips re-shot: every far leg attached, ≥ 3 px of it showing at all 8 keys (≥ 4 on 17). |
| S1 | **Major:** the sleeping young and adult spike is rock's shape at ÷ 3 | Applied | Long and low instead of a quill ball: body level, the tail laid back along the floor top side up, the comb leaning 20° back from the world's upright (4.2 notes, 4.3). Measured at ÷ 3: 3 or 4 teeth at every phase on a bar 3.1 to 3.5 × its height; rock shows 1 or 2 bumps on a dome. |
| S2 | **Major:** baby spike's seed is lost at ÷ 3; baby lightning's two nubs take over the saw | Applied | Spike's nubs 6 tall on a base of 5, points rounded 1 px, loin and rump 6 px apart plus the tail nub (the popped nub of the fizzle matches). Lightning's far nub tucks inside the near one (−1, −1), one spire. Rechecked every block at all three phases: spike's notched top and raised tail, rock's round loaf, lightning's one spire and water's paddle each name the baby. |
| S3 | **Major:** the young spike loses its saw at `mood` −1 | Applied, in part | Capping the thorn shear cannot keep a 3 × 3 notch between 6 px bases 7.6 px apart; the young droops to 36° instead (3.3), 8° under its rest, the most that still reads as a droop. Counted on the ÷ 3 top edge: 3 / 4 / 2 teeth at phases 0 / 1 / 2 (at 50°: 3 / 2 / 2; at the suggested 40°: 3 / 2 / 2; only 32°, 4° under rest, reached 3 at phase 2). Phase 2 shows two teeth and the tail's on a stepped back, no longer a smooth ramp, but not yet a clean saw. |

Ribs from 1–2 px off the root are turned down: 5.2's rib clearance (2 px of membrane round every stamp) leaves nothing drawable at the fan's narrow root, and spreading the adult's ribs wider (tried at −10 / 20 / 50°) culled the outer two against the edges, leaving one. |

**Found while fixing.**
- The far legs' walk shift had to be in the anim, not a rig guard: fire and spike build their walks themselves (the strut, the creep), so `walkAnim` takes the build's dims now and both pass them.
- The resting spread's lean has to count the body's pitch: the wake's play-bow turns the wings 12° forward with the chest, so its flap adds the bow back.
- Lightning's tint on a blue body is grey at any partial alpha (yellow over blue); one frame reads as a flash, two as a ghost.
- Rock's shorter baby tail (and lightning's) made both babies 5 to 8 px shorter; 2.4 is re-measured and its stage-step floors still hold (1.29× length, 1.22× height).
- Engine candidates: none new.

**Known gaps after the review.**
- Nothing consumes `event: 'shriek'` / `'call'`; rock's beg pile is not a prop; the per-pet inputs are fed only by the gallery.
- Babies: at ÷ 3 each seed is 1 to 2 px (spike's nubs, lightning's spire, water's paddle); they now separate by shape, narrowly. The baby far roots lie shallowest (0.5 to 1.5 px), under the head.
- Slinkwing: at rest its adult ribs read as diverging strokes rather than fingers (A10).
- Surprised and scared share one eye on every stage (S10).
- The young spike at `mood` −1: at ÷ 3 phase 2 its back is stepped, two teeth and the tail's, not a clean saw (S3); a hooked thorn (the droop in the tip, the base at the rest lean) was tried and lay flat as flags.
- Rock: the baby's two-tone head; the upset's front wall is a solid dark band.
- Water: happy spots read duller than resting ones (3.6's open proposal).
- The idle personalities (A14) and the rock and water breath look-development (A7).

### Cast review round 2
Two reviews judged the whole cast again after round 1: a silhouette and readability review (**S**: 0 blockers, 1 major, 7 minor, two of them carry-overs) and an art-direction review (**A**: 0 blockers, 0 major, 8 minor). Every item was reproduced on renders before it was changed and looked at again after (the ÷ 3 blocks of `view=silhouette` at `set=low` / `asleep` / `idle`, always at a frozen `t`, over seeds 1–3 and t 0 / 40 / 80 where a count was the test; strips, zooms, stage sheets, the mood sheets, lineups, grey, deuteranopia and the habitat), across all six elements after every shared change. `tsc`, the palette check (280 / 280, re-pasted in 5.8) and the smoke run (38 views) pass, and the floor and leg-root audits find nothing on seeds 1, 2, 3 and 5.

The suggested × 0.74 left the young 2 nicks at phase 2 (4 px teeth). |

**Found while fixing.**

- The idle personalities (A5).

### v2 design round
The user asked for an elder stage, greying with age, holes in elder wings, a spikier spike, the slinkwing rename (done before this round), a dark-blue-to-grey seventh dragon and a sea-green water dragon. Four proposals answered, each prototyped in a scratch copy of the rig (renders in `shots/elder/`, `shots/dusk/`, `shots/dusk_charm/` and `shots/sw/`): **EL** the elder, greying and wing wear; **NA** and **NB** two independent seventh elements; **SW** spike and water. The lead art director took one seventh element and grafted the other onto it, then re-ran every colour through the palette check extended to seven elements, four stages and every pair of stages (2168 gates, 5.8), and built the colour half: `palettes.ts` (the dusk and sea-green palettes, the greying as data and functions), `tools/palette-check.ts`, a first-pass `elements/dusk.ts`, and the gallery's seven-column layouts. `tsc`, the palette check and `npm run smoke` (39 views, the floor and leg-root audits over all seven elements) pass; the lineup was rendered to confirm the sea-green water and the placeholder draw.

| # | Item | From | Verdict | Why, in one line |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| V1 | The elder column of 2.1: not bigger, the head low and forward, the chest settled, a paunch, settled legs, the stretched membrane | EL | Applied (design) | Measured on the prototype: 81 floor-audit rows clean with the beard in the floor guard, the far roots ≥ 2.5 px deep; the head top 7 to 9 px lower (rock 4). |
| V2 | The elder face: the adult eye with no default lid, a 3 px brow ridge, the grey muzzle, the tuft at every face, the beard | EL | Applied (design) | A permanent lid is `sleepy`; the frame around the eye reads "wise" instead (2.5). |
| V3 | Greying: the luminance-keeping transform, G 0 / 0.08 / 0.18 / 0.45, the slot weights | EL | Applied | Keeps every value gate valid by construction (3.9). |
| V4 | EL's per-element strengths (fire 0.4 / −0.6, spike +0.2, rock 0.55, dusk s 2.0) and SW's water drift −0.3 | EL, SW | **Changed** | Checked within a stage only. Across stages they failed 10 gates with the sea-green water (the greyed rock against water under protanopia, 6; the elder water against fire under deuteranopia, 4), and dusk at k 0.9 went neutral. Now fire 0.3 / −0.6, spike 0.9 / +0.2, rock 0.25 on a warmer base (D28), water 0.8, dusk its own curve to S 0.24. |
| V5 | Fire / spike in greyscale | lead | **Accepted as E10** | No strengths keep both the protanopia step and the greyscale step once spike greys visibly (3.9); the protanopia gate is kept. |
| V6 | Wing wear: tears at `wing` ≥ 0.55, holes only at full spread, cut through the far wing; lightning's chip and tear; none on rock | EL | Applied (design) | The measured clear membrane (2.9). |

| V8 | Each element's elder cue and elder-only extra | EL | Applied, **except** spike's extra tail quill at 0.8 of the tail | The last 12 px of a spike tail stay a plain taper (D5, water's zone); SW's elder comb (one taller, one chipped quill) takes its place, with EL's sap-buds. |
| V9 | The seventh element | NA, NB | **NB's "Wisp", with NA's grafts** | D24: the same free zone, found independently; NB reads "dark blue → grey" both ways; NA's cap, mist breath, `hush` and elder moth grafted on. |
| V10 | NB's palette as hand-set per-stage hexes | NB | Applied, **as a curve** | Nearly the same values (within 0.03 of saturation, the same luminance) come from the greying transform on dusk's own curve, so its stages are derived like every other element's (D22). |

| V12 | Dusk's markings: NA's crescent moons or NB's smoke tail tip and nose frost | NA, NB | NB's | The crescents risked reading as slinkwing's arcs or as text; the grey tip and frost are "dark blue → grey" along the body, and the frost grows into the elder muzzle. |

**Found while applying.**
- A within-stage colour check is not enough: an elder shares the habitat with babies, and the first cross-stage run failed pairs that passed at every matched stage. Gates (b) and (f) now run at all 16 stage combinations.
- Greying that keeps luminance moves a red toward its true luminance under protanopia (its lost red returns as grey), and a green away from it: fire and spike can hold their protanopia step or their greyscale step, not both (3.9).
- The sea-green water simulates to a near-grey under both dichromacies; its pairs with fire (deuteranopia) and the greyed rock (protanopia) pass by chroma, B3, with the least room in the cast (margins 1.17 and 1.04).
- `DRAGON_PALETTES` stays the base (hatchling) palette, so every existing import still draws; `dragonTones` gained an optional stage.
- Engine candidates: none new.

**Known gaps after v2 (the implementation phase; the elder core has since closed the elder's shared half: see the elder core review below).**
- The elder in the rig: `Stage` and `STAGE_DIMS.elder` (2.1, with `chestLift`), the elder timing (4.1), the elder anims (4.2), the face (2.5), wing wear (2.9), every element's elder column and extra (section 3), the gallery's elder row, and 2.4's elder sizes measured on the drawn rig.
- The rig drawing each stage through `agedPalette` (build.ts), and water's `DIM` and rock's `PAL` reads moved to the rig's palette.
- Dusk's element pass: the lamp, the nose frost, the `tip` marking kind, the Nightfall breath and its fizzle, the moths, the lamp-bat fidget, its 4.3 column and its placeholder replaced; its sizes re-measured.
- Spike's new comb in `spike.ts` (COMB, the elder entry, the chip, the 5 × 10 thrown nub, the volley of 4) and `parts.ts` letting the elder through its adult-only wing thorns.
- Nothing consumes `event: 'shriek'`, `'call'` or `'hush'`; rock's beg pile is not a prop; the per-pet inputs are fed only by the gallery.
- Carried over: surprised and scared share one eye; water's happy spots read duller than resting ones (3.6); the idle personalities (cast review round 2, A5).

### Review log (v2)
Two critiques reviewed the v2 design: **VR**, readability (2 blockers, 4 major, 7 minor), and **VC**, the care game (1 blocker, 14 major, 10 minor). Every item was checked against the files first: the colour claims re-run with the critics' scratch scripts and with `palettes.ts` itself, the geometry claims from the bible's own numbers and the code (build.ts `lenVar`, spike.ts `backRow`, the stalk's Bézier), the look claims on the renders (`shots/elder/`, the lineup). Every measured claim reproduced. The palette check (**2251 of 2251**, re-pasted in 5.8: 83 gates more than v2's 2168, for the dark-pair floor, the elder face and extras, the floor contacts and visible greying), `tsc` and `npm run smoke` (39 views, the floor and leg-root audits over all seven elements) pass; the dusk placeholder's new build was also run through both audits on seeds 1, 2, 3 and 5 (shallowest far root 0.8 px, nothing under the floor). **Applied** means the change is in this bible, and in `palettes.ts`, `palette-check.ts` or the placeholder `elements/dusk.ts` where it touches them; the rest (the elder in the rig, dusk's renderers, spike's comb) is design for the implementation phase.

|---|---|---|---|

**Found while applying.**
- The proposed petrol navy `#2f5571` fixed the lightning pair but greyed to a near-slate from hatching (Oklab chroma 0.064, the old navy's 0.113); a sweep of the free value slot in Oklab LCh found `#1f5580` holds the floor to lightning in all three visions with more chroma, so the blue-to-grey journey stays visible.
- The dark-pair floor can only be gated in normal vision: under protanopia the v1 pair fire / spike already sits under it (5.4 Oklab L), the classic red–green confusion; both colour-blind cases are reported (E13).
- Setting the face greys by luminance exposed that rock's muzzle and beard have disjoint windows (a pale skull patch, a mid-grey beard), and that water's only window is a near-white frost.
- The new lantern hangs longer than NB's disc and the 0.8 snout sits further back, so every stage's P values moved (3.8); a grid search on the stalk's Bézier met the rise budgets with 0.5 px to spare, the happy lift and the 4 px clearance together.
- A `glow.hi` glint on a `glow` bud (spike's first sap-buds) is 5 % apart, invisible: the buds are `glow.sh` with a `glow` glint.
- `palettes.ts` gained `AGE_SILVER`, `AgeRule.from`, `atLum`, `beardOf`, `MUZZLE_LUM` / `BEARD_LUM` and `smokeBandOf`; the rig is untouched (it asks `dragonTones` for the baby palette only until the elder is built, where it will seed every slot).
- Engine candidates: none new.

**Known gaps after the v2 review (the implementation phase; superseded, for the elder, by the gaps after the elder core review below).**
- Everything listed after the v2 design round, plus: the airing variant and its per-element lean; the whole-pixel hole stamp; the beard as its own inked tuft; the lantern, bail and finial; the resident moth; the coal bed, sap-buds, pearls and third tooth; spike's revised comb and the `lenVar` clamp in `backRow`; slinkwing's slink walk.
- To measure on the drawn rig: the elder sizes and ÷ 3 read with the higher head, the ÷ 3 slinkwing / dusk A/B, the elder dusk's lamp clearance at ÷ 3, the lantern with naive viewers, spike's notch counts, the third tooth at ÷ 3, and the elder dusk's neutral area.
- For the user: the water swatches (3.6), and the names "dusk" and "Wick".

### Elder core review round 1
The elder core (the implementation phase's shared half: the elder in the rig's `Stage` and every per-stage table, each stage drawn through `agedPalette`, the elder posture, face, wing wear, the elder column of the shared anims with its variants, every element file giving its elder valid params, the gallery's elder row and `view=wings`) was reviewed on the drawn rig (the reviewer's scratch shots `shots/review-elder-*.png`). The reviewer confirmed what holds: every elder reads older at game scale and not as an adult, the greying steps show (the young untouched), elders stay distinct in the grey and cvd views, every ÷ 3 silhouette keeps its element's shape, the size caps hold, the elder timing matches 4.2 with no key holds, the airing is charming, and the adult fire rest silhouette is pixel-identical to the pre-elder baseline. Every item below was reproduced by rendering it first (`shots/ecr1/`: the resting-spread and full-spread wings at pixel zoom, each tear rendered with and without its cut, the beards counted at scale 1, the belly counted and its shadow read from `dragonTones`, the sizes on `view=zoom` at scale 1). **Fixed** means the change is in the code and in this bible. `npm run check` passes: tsc, the palette check **2251 of 2251** (re-pasted in 5.8: rock's elder belly shadow and water's beard changed) and the smoke run (48 views, the floor and leg-root audits over all 28 looks).

| # | Item | Verdict | Why, in one line |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | **Blocker:** every elder cue and elder-only extra is missing, so the last grow-up only greys the dragon and tears its wings | **Rejected here: the element pass's** | The elder core's brief kept them out of the shared rig on purpose ("do not build element-specific elder extras, the spike comb change, or dusk's renderers"): each is its element's renderer (3.2 to 3.8), each spot is marked "FIRST PASS (elder)" with its section, and the seam hands the element artists everything they need (`grown`, `info.pal` and `rig.greys` already greyed, `WingParams.tears`, `pathTearEdge` and `wearOf` for lightning's bolt tear, `ACT.airing`, the breath's finale window). The status line (top) now says the elder's reward (D15, VC1) is unfinished until that pass. |
| E2 | Fire's elder fidget ends dizzy: 4 hopping paper turns, then `dazed`, stars and a wobble | Fixed | The elder's chase makes 2 half-turns (at the chase's first and third beats), no hops, its eyes `happy`, the tail and the look back at 0.8×, and stops content: no `dazed`, no stars, no wobble (D21, VC14); 94 f (× 1.3). |
| E3 | Rock's and dusk's beards are specks under the mark floor (3 and ≈ 5 px) | Fixed, **at the root** | One fixed beard shape hung under every jaw, and a skull hanging lower than its jaw (rock's boxy snout, dusk's short one) covered it. The rig fits each head at build (`parts.ts fitBeard`): the beard's lower points hang deeper until ≥ 3 × 3 px of fill show below the skull's ink with the jaw shut (0.5 to 2.25 px). Counted at scale 1 (the beard's exact colour): fire 12, spike 12, rock 10, lightning 12, water 12, slinkwing 11, dusk 9 (were 8, 8, 1, 10, 8, 8, 2). A per-element offset in the seam, as proposed, would have to be re-tuned whenever a snout changes. |
| E4 | Rock's elder belly reads as a grey nappy | Fixed | Its hand-set warm belly shadow greys at 0.5 (`AgeRule.shadow`), not the cream's 1.5: the elder's was a neutral `#dbd5c5` (gate a's scale.sh / belly.sh had lost its hue), now `#e2d4ac`; rock's elder keeps the adult's 38 % band (`ElementStageParams.bellyFrac`). Gates (a), (i) and (k) pass. The band is still the adult's plus the paunch (145 px against 107), which is the elder's posture signal (2.1). |
| E5 | The tears do not read at the resting spread | Fixed, **in part** | Three causes, not one: the notch narrowed to a 1.5 px bottom (now a slot square to its mouth: 3 px of background over its top half, 2 over its bottom), its mouth opened 1.3 px from a spar tip and cut along under the bone (now it keeps 3 px of edge from each tip), and every near cut showed the far wing's darker membrane behind it (now the far wing is cut under the near tears). Background the tears open at the resting spread (0.6 / 48), each rendered with and without its cut: fire 10 px, dusk 12, spike's bite 7, a notch at game scale each (the slot alone opened 5 on fire). The reviewer's `mx ± 1.5 u` bottom would have shown 1 px of background, not 3: the ink takes 1 px on each side. **Not fixed:** water's fin opens 0 px and slinkwing 3 at the resting spread, 4-spar panels being ≤ 3 px (2.9's own measure); theirs read at the airing and in flight. |
| E6 | The idle "hmm" reads as a gasp or a cough | Fixed | The jaw stays shut; the hmm is a 3° nod, a 1 px swell of the chest, `happy` for 12 f and one slow sweep of the tail; 4.2 amended. |
| E7 | The hole reads as a ringed dot | Fixed as proposed | The ring is left open at the notch pixel. At game scale the 3 × 3 window is still a small pale spot; see "found" below. |
| E8 | Water's near-white beard reads as a buck tooth | Fixed | `BEARD_LUM.water` 0.32, a mid sea-slate `#969a99` in its 0.29 to 0.36 window (belly 34 %, belly shadow 32 %, scale shadow 51 %, floor 53 %: its beard / floor was the cast's thinnest, 27 %), a mid grey like every other element's beard; its other window, a near-black ≤ 0.11, would have been the one dark beard in the cast. |
| E9 | The muzzle leaves a 1 px rim of scale | Fixed | The muzzle bitmap takes every pixel the skull touches and is clipped to the skull path (drawn in cranium space, entering face space under the clip), so it meets the skull's ink as the scale does. |
| E10 | Water's elder head sits 6 px low | Fixed | Its neck carried 4° higher (`neckAngle` +4; +2 raised the head about half a pixel, under the rounding): head top 4 px under the adult's, length 129 (× 1.05). |
| E11 | The elder fidgets run at × 1.25 and full amplitude | Fixed (lightning excepted) | `FIDGET_TIMING` (stages.ts): the elder's × 1.3 and 0.8×, read by every fidget and by each renderer that keys on the fidget's clock (rock's dust, spike's pecks, slinkwing's pings, water's drops); the amplitude on the gestures, never on a reach the fidget needs (spike's snout on its comb, rock's belly on the floor, slinkwing's fan flare, the cue). Lightning's zoomies already play 4.2's own elder numbers, 28 px in 60 f. |
| E12 | Rock's airing shows only a defect (its stub under the rim) | Fixed | Rock has no hole to air: its elder pool reminisces in the airing's place (`idleVariants(stage, wing)`), and its airing, if played, keeps the wings folded under the rim. |
| E13 | Loose ends: no breath finale ring; rock's elder beg 120 f; grow-up and fly unbuilt | Rock's beg fixed; the rest left | Rock's beg is 140 f (every beat × 140 / 120, the growl where the shared beg's lands). The finale ring (optional in 4.2) is each element's breath renderer (the anim hands it the window); the grow-up and the gliding flight were not in the elder core's brief. |
| E14 | The bible is out of date with the code | Fixed | The status line and 1.1's file notes, 2.4's elder row (the drawn rig, seed 1), 2.9's hole positions (as built) and tear construction, 2.5's face greys, 3.9's rock row, 4.1 and 4.2; the known-gaps lists above point here. |

**Found while applying.**
- The tear's own geometry allocated two closures per call in a renderer (`edgeTornTo`): now inline.
- Rock's adult belly shadow moves with E4 too, warmer (`#e2d4af` → `#e5d4a4`), since its shadow weight applies at every stage; gate (d) holds it 27 % under the belly at all four.
- The hole, even open at its notch, is a pale 3 × 3 spot ringed in ink sitting in the middle of the arm panel: at game scale it can still read as an eyespot. Seating the window on the forearm bone (2.9 lets the bone be one border), so it breaks the membrane's edge beside the bone, is the next thing to try; its position is element data.
- Slinkwing's elder silhouette is 8 px lower than its adult's while its head is only 5 lower: its fans set its height, and they grow to 19 px with its elder cue (3.7).
- Rock's wake stretch (adult and elder alike) spreads its stub to the resting spread for 12 f, a smaller slate wedge under the rim; left to rock's element pass (4.3).

**Known gaps after the elder core (the element pass; superseded by the gaps after round 2 below).** Every elder cue and elder-only extra (the FIRST PASS notes in each element file), lightning's bolt tear, the breath's finale ring, water's and slinkwing's tears at the resting spread (a wider elder spread for 4-spar wings, or tears on their arm panel, to decide there), the hole's eyespot read, dusk's renderers, spike's comb, the grow-up and the gliding flight; then the ÷ 3 sheet and the palette gates (a) and (h) again with the extras drawn.

### Elder core review round 2
The reviewer re-checked round 1 on the drawn rig (`shots/review-elder2-*.png`) and found no regressions (the t = 0 lineup's baby, young and adult rows pixel-identical to round 1) and round 1's items #2, #3, #4, #6 and #8 to #12 fixed; the six items below were open. Each was reproduced by rendering it first (`shots/ecr2/`: the airing's sit-back F0 to F104 on every holed elder and lightning, the tail's height every 2 f against a copy of the round-1 code, the hole at 8× and at game scale beside the round-1 hole, the tears' background counted with and without the wear through the gallery's new `wear=0`, rock's wake at 4×). **Fixed** means the change is in the code and in this bible. `npm run check` passes: tsc, the palette check **2251 of 2251** (no colour changed) and the smoke run (48 views, the floor and leg-root audits over all 28 looks). Regressions, measured against the round-1 code on the t = 0, 12, 24, 45, 90 and 150 lineups of all nine core anims (54 sheets, per cell): every cell pixel-identical but rock's wake (young, adult and elder: its stub no longer leaves the rim) and the elder water's preen and wake (its tear moved: E2-3).

**Found while applying.**
- A gallery view without `t` runs live: the wings view's airing row, seeked to its hold, kept stepping before the shot, so two measures of the same code differed. Measure with `t=0`; `wear=0` (new) draws the elders without their tears and hole, for the with / without counts.
- A tear's notch between two finger bones is filled by the bones themselves (painted into the wing's one silhouette), so on a narrow panel a deeper tear opens no more of the room: slinkwing's panel 1 at depth 7 opened what depth 5 did.
- The hole's clearance test (`armPanelHas`) knows the trailing edge, not the tears; no element tears its arm panel (2.9's table), so none can meet the window. An element that tears it later needs the notch in the test.
- Engine candidates: none new.

**Known gaps after the elder core, round 2 (the element pass).** Every elder cue and elder-only extra (the FIRST PASS notes in each element file), lightning's bolt tear, the breath's finale ring (required: 4.2), slinkwing's tears at the resting spread (no room on its four-finger wing: 2.9), dusk's renderers, spike's comb, the grow-up and the gliding flight; then the ÷ 3 sheet and the palette gates (a) and (h) again with the extras drawn.

### Element pass v2
Each element's v2 work (its elder cue and elder-only extra, its finale ring, and for dusk the whole element) was built by its own artist in its own worktree (branch `elem2/<id>`), critiqued once by a reviewer on the renders, and fixed there, touching only its `elements/<id>.ts`: dusk `48c1671`, fire `e74bc63`, spike `9274556`, rock `0a2a696`, lightning `f73fc5c`, water `b8211c1`, slinkwing `01a510a`. The seven files were integrated as they are onto the elder core (`142eea9`); the integrator's own edits in them are small and named below (dusk declares its `reach`; fire, water, rock and slinkwing key their rings from `ELDER_FINALE`; slinkwing's wake override is dropped, the shared wake having taken its lean, its wing keys identical frame for frame; spike's and dusk's ring comments note that their rings open 6 f before the window). What each element now draws is in sections 2.9, 3 and 4, from the artists' notes of both rounds. The artists' SHARED requests were then applied or turned down, each looked at on renders before and after: the lineup (idle at t 0 and 45, walk, happy, wake, breath, sleep) diffed cell by cell against the seven files on the unchanged shared code, the silhouette sheet, the habitat and its mix, `view=wings`, every stage sheet, the face sheets, and the strips and zooms named below. The lineup diffs changed only what each request should: at idle, breath and sleep only rock's elder cell (its muzzle); in the walk and the happy only elder cells (the tail held stiff); in the wake every cell (the tail held through the shake; the lean). `npm run check` passes: tsc, the palette check **2252 of 2252** (one new gate: slinkwing's frost / `membrane.sh`) and the smoke run (**53 views**: lightning's storm-watch and turning ring, rock's sunning, dusk's face sheet and its tuck-in added), the floor audit over every look's fidget at every stage and dusk's `tuckin` (401 look × anim runs, none sinking), and the leg-root audit; `npm run shots` renders its 201 sheets.

**The element reviews** (one critique per element, then its artist's fixes; the details are in 2.9, 3.2 to 3.8 and 4.2 to 4.3):
- **Fire:** the hearth read as a crown (a box with three near-even points, a straight coal band and two jewel specks): a round bottom, unmatched side licks, a one-tongue heart standing on the bed, the bed a bowl, specks never a level pair and one lit with the core out. The ring now leaves an all-fire jet, the one smoke in the air, and never ends as a pill.
- **Spike:** the back sap-bud flickered under the folded wing (2 to 4 of 9 px through the idle): reseated behind the rump quill, 9 / 9 px on every frame of every anim and mood; the placement rule amended to "nestled"; the tail bud lowered (it, not the back bud, cost a ÷ 3 notch); the ring opens at cue 32 out of the sparkle and fades by narrowing; the quill mark floor; the baby's rump nub moved so every frame keeps a notch; the counts recorded (3.3).
- **Rock:** the roar's root and head jitter were the tremor D21 bans (now the shared recoil and one head swell); the sunning sat too close to the sunbathe (now it sits up to 18°, open-eyed, charging its crystals to one all-tips glint); the crystals stood level as a comb (the outermost sunk and fanned, the growth order kept together); the elder upset trembled (now a sulk breath at its tempo); the ring stands alone.
- **Lightning:** the near-bolt tear read as a pinhole, an eye in the spire (moved to the far bolt's trailing edge, opened only in the storm-watch's V); the storm-watch barely moved (chest 8°, snout 18° up, eyes open, the far bolt fanned 32°, a two-rung ladder that breaks off the top); the idle arc read as a hoop (a 3-hop spark); the finale ring turns 45° a step.
- **Water:** the elder fluke had lost its rays and looked plainer than the adult's (the rays are back, running into the pearls with membrane between them, the pearls shaded as round beads); the happy bubbles were born on the snout and the third never popped on the young and baby (born ahead of the tip, fanned, per-stage timing); the adult's glow dot lost a corner to the fluke's clip.
- **Slinkwing:** the finger-panel tear never opened more than an ink tick (dropped: one arm-panel tear at 0.55 and the hole moved to the wrist); the airing sits back 28° so the tear opens on the room; the preen stays 0.9 / 60° (its tear there shows the flank, by necessity: measured); the freeze's fans lift half way, not upright; the echo circle runs 22 f and narrows out; the elder beg chirps every 70 f; the young slinks as low as the adult.
- **Dusk** (first built in this pass): Nightfall fell as separate discs and piled into a stone (one billowing column that lays one long, low bank); the elder's ring now rises off the bank's front; the resident moth filled the bail's gap and the lantern read as a spray can (a delta on the cap's front end, 1 px clear); the lamp-bat stood up by folding its tail and its swipes waved at nothing (a chin-tucked sit that dangles the lamp at the paw, contact at f 25, a 19 f stand-up with the tail held; the baby's is a miss and a topple, its bud out of any reach); the mood crescent and the nightlight sliver held to 2 px; the motes stopped clumping (a quarter turn apart on one orbit under the lamp); the lantern turns only in the lamp-bat; the baby's nightlight moved out and its beg bows; the finial narrowed and 2 rows of bail show; the clearance measured, not guessed.

- Spike: the elder's deep notches are 0.1 to 0.2 under the same elder with no buds; D26's 2.6 deep notches at `mood` −1 is 1.5 built; every cue-keyed effect steps at 30 fps (#7).

- Nothing consumes `event: 'shriek'`, `'call'` or `'hush'`, and nothing plays `tuckin`: the game's care loop does not exist yet.

### Cast review v2 round 1
The first cast-level review of the integrated v2 cast: all 28 looks judged together by two reviewers, **appeal and consistency** (4 majors, 6 minors) and **distinctness** (2 majors, 8 minors), with four items the lead added: (a) the dusk stage sheet cut the elder's lantern at its edge, (b) a small grey shape by the ADULT Wick's lamp, (c) the elder Ripple's stark white muzzle, (d) a chunk cut out of the elder Zap's back bolt. A first fixer took the round most of the way (the tail-ceiling audit and `tailRise`, the airing tuning group, the brow tuft's own grey, dusk's lantern, moth, iris, tip, core and belly, the sap-buds, the silvering, the rock crystal and the fire ring) and was stopped by a container restart; a second finished it, checked every item on renders and did what was still open. Every item was looked at on a render before and after (`shots/cf1/`: the lineup at t 0, the silhouette sheets with `set=all`, grey, cvd, the habitat idle and its mix at t 60 and 200, every element's stage sheet, the elder face sheets, the elder cast at 4×, `view=wings`, and the breath, airing, fidget and walk strips below); `npm run check` passes (tsc, **2258 of 2258** palette gates, 55 smoke views with the floor, leg-root, tail-ceiling and neutral-area audits).

| # | Item | Verdict | What changed, and what it looks like now |
|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | **Major:** Wick's Nightfall reads as being sick (eyes shut, jaw 26° with the tongue, one lumpy column from the lower lip to a pile on the floor) | **Fixed** | The tell and the pour are `happy`, the jaw at its stage minimum (16°, young 20°). The lobes are born 9 px out, level under the lamp's foot (2 px of air), and **blown along one arc** that sinks as it slows, landing 28 to 34 px ahead of the mouth, r 1.5 → 2.5 in the air, the bank taking the volume and lying under and ahead of the lamp (3.8). The strips (young, adult, elder) show a ribbon of mist curling out and down, never a column from the lip; a first try that glided 3 px and fell 12 px ahead of the mouth was still a column under the lamp. |
| A2 | **Major:** the elder beard reads as a grey lump under the jaw | **Fixed** | Two changes (2.5): the muzzle grey **wraps the chin**, the jaw's front 7 px (pigment clipped to the jaw, open or shut), so the beard grows from a grey chin, not from under a chin in the scale's colour; and the tuft is **swept back**, deepest at its back lobe, its tip trailing 9 px behind the chin past its own root, inked on its outer contour only. At 1× on all seven it reads as a grey chin with a goatee; the first fix's shape (two lobes deepest at the front, the chin still red) was still a lump. |
| A3 | **Major:** the elder's resident moth doesn't read (a nozzle on the cap's corner) | **Fixed** | It sits on the glass at the front edge, head up, wings folded in a tent, grey on the coral inside the lantern's outline, and flicks open 12 f in 36 (3.8). |
| A4 | **Major:** the Wick elder is the drabbest look, faded rather than silvered | **Fixed** | Its belly greys at a quarter (the afterglow stays warm), the smoke tip is 11 px (not 16), and the full-moon core glows at any `mood` ≥ 0 (3.8, 3.9). The neutral-area recorder (`view=neutral`, 5.5, in the smoke run) puts it at 32 % (spike's elder 36 %, all 28 ≤ 40 %). |
| A5 | The lantern reads as a capsule or a vial | Fixed | The cap is a roof, its eave a pixel over the body each side; the bail is an ink arch with background inside (3.8). |
| A6 | The Wick baby is the least baby-like silhouette; its bud a lure | Fixed | Tail × 0.9, the curl drawn in to the 3 px clearance, the bud's top a flat lid (3.8). |
| A7 | The Wick baby's eye looks teary | Fixed | Iris `#6f86ff`, a deep periwinkle, 70 % from the catchlight (3.1, 3.8). |
| A8 | Ripple's near-white muzzle and tuft are the brightest marks on the elder | Fixed (with lead item c) | The tuft is its own pale grey (0.60) and 5 px with rounded ends (rock's 0.68); the muzzle, chin and beard a pale sea frost, 0.65 `#d1d3d2`, not the near-white 0.92 (2.5). Its beard sits 3 % from the straw and is exempt, inked (5.6 E14). |
| A9 | Bramble's sap-buds read as square rivets | Fixed | Round 4 × 4 buds, lit over a calyx row (3.3). |
| A10 | At full spread the wing stands as a narrow column | **Deferred** | Only the unbuilt flight uses the full spread; fan the spars 15 to 25° and keep the tips inside the scallop line when flight is built. |
| D1 | **Major:** water's tail rises into fire's zone in the core anims | **Fixed** | `ElementStageParams.tailRise` (element.ts, rig.ts): a look takes only that share of any swing that would lift its tail over its rest line, and all of the droop; water's is 0 at every stage. The tail-ceiling audit (`view=tails`, smoke) measures every look's core anims frame by frame: water ≤ 2 px over the back everywhere (was up to 26), nothing gated over 3. |
| D2 | **Major:** the fire elder's airing lays its flame on the floor (water's airing at ÷ 3) | **Fixed** | `tuning.airing.tailLift` (−1 for fire's elder): the tail keeps its up-curled line through the sit, the flame standing over the rump behind the haunch; beside water's airing at ÷ 3 (the hold, box-filtered) fire's tail ends up in a knob, water's lies along the floor to its fluke. |
| D3 | The lamp-bat closes the crook into a loop against the snout | Fixed | 2.5 px of background kept to the snout and jaw outside the bonk, the lamp dangled 3 px further out through the sit (3.8). |
| D4 | Lightning's and slinkwing's elders barely grey | Fixed | Their elders' highlight band silvers at 0.8 and their bellies grey at 1.5 (3.9); gates b, f and k pass. |
| D5 | Water's beard is a pebble in the mouth too | Fixed | With A2 and A8: one pale frost grown from a frost chin. |
| D6 | The elder lantern plus moth reads as a spray bottle | Fixed | With A3 and A5. |
| D7 | Asleep, the rock elder's four crystals make a crown of teeth | Fixed | Asleep the fourth sits low on the dome's rear rim: ≤ 2 points at the apex at ÷ 3 (3.4). |
| D8 | The habitat has only two elders | Fixed | 12 dragons: the dark trio's elders held at the back (lightning's behind slinkwing's, dusk's facing them, spaced by its `reach`), the two airings and the lamp-bat in the mix (5.4); raised 14 px so dusk's elder clears the spike elder's comb. |
| D9 | Asleep, the fire and spike babies are the same bun at ÷ 3 | Fixed | Spike's baby lays its tail out level (lift 0): a flat tail and a nub against fire's raised knob (4.2). |
| D10 | Fire's and dusk's elder finale rings are both grey | Fixed | Fire's ring has a 1 px `glow` ember rim while round (3.2). |
| a | The stage sheet cuts the elder Wick's lantern | Fixed | `view=stages` adds 4 px of margin each side and ≥ 8 px of straw between stages; measured on all seven sheets (and dusk's at t 50 and 130), ≥ 37 px of straw at both edges at the sheet's scale 3 (12 game px). `view=zoom` also widens by the reach. |

The open glyph is now a raised V of wings (10001 / 11011 / 01110), the ink ring its body; a top view with a body read as a broken heart. |

Its sides now meet in a point, a rip, with the stepped lower side (3.5). |

**Found while fixing.**
- The zoom view cut the elder dusk's lantern at its right edge (now widened by the reach).
- `npm run check` gates 2258 (was 2259 in the first fixer's run): the water beard's floor gate is reported as exempt, not counted.
- In hi-res renders (strip scale 3) the grey chin and swept tuft read as a full grey beard under an open jaw; at 1× a grey chin and goatee. Rock's stays two-tone (a white chin, a mid-stone tuft: its beard window is mid, 2.5).
- The visiting moth appears on stage sheets and lineups whenever a pet's schedule puts it in flight at the frozen frame (by design; the same seed puts one by the adult Wick at t 0).
- On an open jaw the grey chin now meets the jaw's belly half (the chin stripe), a pair no gate lists: 25 to 70 % apart on six elders, 5 % on rock (its white chin running into its cream stripe, which reads as one pale chin).

**Still open after this round.** A10 (the full-spread fan, with the flight). Spike's and dusk's finale rings still open 6 f before the window. The adult preen still flicks the long tails (the tail-ceiling audit gates only a tip shape). The known gaps of the Element pass v2 otherwise stand.

### Cast review v2 round 2
The second cast-level review of the v2 cast, after round 1's fixes: the same two reviewers, **distinctness** (1 major, 5 minors: D1 to D6) and **appeal and consistency** (1 major, 4 minors: A1 to A5), both with round 1's items re-checked on renders (distinctness: D1 to D10 and a to d all fixed or improved; appeal: all fixed but the moth, improved, and Nightfall, not fixed). Every item was looked at on a render before and after (`shots/cf2/`: the habitat mix at t 60, 200 and 520 in colour, greyscale and deuteranopia with the dark trio cropped at 2×, Nightfall's young, adult and elder strips frame by frame, the elder ring, the adult Wick's happy, the tail-ceiling audit, the elder Cobble's head before and after, the elder faces, the stage sheets of rock, lightning and slinkwing, the elder cast sheet, the resident moth at pixel zoom, spike's elder ring; then the lineup, grey, cvd and the full `npm run shots` set); `npm run check` passes (tsc, **2255 of 2255** palette gates, 56 smoke views with the floor, leg-root, tail-ceiling, **pour-column** and neutral-area audits).

| # | Item | Verdict | What changed, and what it looks like now |
|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | **Major:** Nightfall still reads as the dragon throwing up: one beaded rope from just ahead of the open mouth (fangs and tongue showing, eyes `^`) in an arc to a puddle, its tail standing on the pile | **Fixed** | Rebuilt as **breathed out, laid out, then let settle** (3.8): three puffs of 3 lobes (young two), 6 f apart, blown **level** from 5 px out into one band of cloud at snout height under the lamp, the first puff furthest out; from cue 27 the band **settles as one sheet** (every lobe at the same rate over 20 f, eased in and out, the front puff 1 f first), swelling and flattening into the bank. The mouth is only **parted**, 9° (`ElementStageParams.jawPart`, a new rig option: under the stage minimum, no tongue, no fangs), the happy eyes and blush kept. No stalk (nothing falls onto the bank; the band is in the air until it is all down). A new audit, `view=pour` (5.5), fails any breath frame with one effect mark from 3 px under the mouth to the floor: round 2's rope fails it on all three Wicks, the new one passes on all 28 looks. |
| A2 | Rock's elder beard is still a separate grey lump under its white chin | Fixed | The beard is the muzzle white (`BEARD_LUM` rock removed): the white chin grows a white tuft, an old dog's. It sits 5 % from the cream stripe and 22 % from the straw, inked on its outer contour: 5.6 **E15** (the palette check reports its three pairs as exempt). |
| A3 | The resident moth reads as a grey mark on the glass, not a creature | Fixed | The closed tent has a 1 px ink body down its middle column (rows 1 to 3): two folded wings round a body. The 12-in-36 flick stays, and it also flicks once as a pet begins (a tap's answer). |
| A4 | Zap's and Echo's elder bodies are still the adult's colour at game scale | Improved | Their elder scales grey a step more (lightning 0.5 → 0.65, slinkwing 1 → 1.2; 3.9): adult → elder 0.029 and 0.038 Oklab ΔE on the scale (were 0.018, 0.028), lineup chroma −23 % and −32 % (were −19, −27). Lightning cannot go further: at 0.75 its elder comes within 0.091 of the baby Wick (it is 0.095 now; the bible's "≥ 0.106 at every stage pair" was the report's thinnest pair and never the least, 0.102: corrected in D25, 3.1, 3.8 and 5.1). The bolts keep it vivid by design. |
| A5 | The adult happy whips the long tails (Wick's from the floor to 25° up in 4 f, its smoke tip kinking up) | Fixed (dusk) | With D4's `tailRise` the lift is 30 %, and dusk's happy (young and older) holds `tail.stiff` ≥ 0.6 through it: the tail sways as one piece, under the back line. Water's, milder, was left (its `tailRise` is 0). |
| D1 | **Major:** in the habitat mix the lightning and slinkwing elders air their wings together and merge into one two-headed dragon (in greyscale and deuteranopia) | **Fixed** | **The crowd rule** (5.4): the owner marks a pet `crowded` when the wary gap is under 24 px (`CROWD_GAP`), and the player's idle schedule then starts no spread-wing variant (`SPREAD_VARIANTS`: the airing; `DragonAnimPlayer.crowded`, `setVariants(…, spread)`). **The check:** lightning's elder at x 286 (not 318: at 300 its snout still touched the fans), slinkwing's playing `reminisce` beside its storm-watch. `post=grey` / `post=cvd` post-process any view; the mix at t 60, 200 and 520 reads as two dragons in all three. The adult's preen spreads too: its crowd rule belongs to the care loop that starts it (not built). |
| D2 | Nightfall lands so far from Wick that another dragon (the adult water) can look like its owner | Fixed | With A1 the bank lies from under the head to about 22 px ahead of the mouth, the lamp over its front half; nothing stands above it. In the habitat the adult dusk stands 6 px higher and the water pair 8 px lower: the bank lies under Wick's own head, ≥ 30 px over the water's. |
| D3 | Rock's elder is barely greyer than its adult (chroma −15 %; gate k 0.007 / 0.037) | Fixed | Its elder's highlight band silvers at 0.8 and its dome greys at 1.3 (3.9): crown 0.057 ΔE, chroma −18 %, the dome stone grey `#706359`. Gates a (the dim crystal on the dome), f (protan rock / water, ΔS 0.31 unchanged) and k pass. The sand itself cannot move (gate f). |
| D4 | In the adult happy Wick's tail lifts 7 px above its back (sleep 6) | Fixed | Dusk's `tailRise` 0.3 at every stage, and the tail-ceiling audit now gates any look that caps its rise (`tailRise` < 1): dusk ≤ −3 px everywhere. Slinkwing's thin tail left as accepted in round 1. |
| D5 | Spike's elder finale ring ends as a 1 px outline | Fixed | It ends on its last 2 px frame (the glow 1 px between its edges, cue 40 to 41) and is gone at cue 42. |
| D6 | 3.9 still quotes the pre-fix numbers | Fixed | 3.9's strengths, values, saturations, gate k and result lines re-measured (with this round's changes); 5.8 re-pasted. |

**Found while fixing.**
- The bank's shrink ran front-first in the first rebuild (its lobes indexed front to back); it shrinks rearmost first, as 3.8 says.
- The elder finale ring, now opened after the sheet is down (cue 45), sat on the bank's end for its first frames and read as a tyre on a puddle: it lifts off at once (8 px × √u).
- Nightfall's round-1 ring timing item ("opens 6 f before the window") is closed for dusk: cue 45 to 57, in the window and the recover's grace. Spike's still opens at cue 32.
- The bible's lightning / dusk ΔE (0.106) was the report's thinnest-pair figure, not the least: the least was 0.102 (elder Zap / baby Wick), now 0.095 (B2's floor is 0.08).
- Nightfall runs longer (young 79 f, adult 123, elder 123; were 75, 101, 109): the band has to settle before the bank and the motes.

**Still open after this round.** A10 (the full-spread fan, with the flight). Spike's elder ring opens 6 f before the window. The adult preen's crowd rule is the care loop's (not built). Zap's elder stays the most saturated elder (its bolts carry it; gate b caps its scale). The known gaps of the Element pass v2 otherwise stand.
